<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

// 🔒 Keamanan: Hanya untuk Role Orang Tua (ortu)
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || $_SESSION['role'] !== 'ortu') {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$id_ortu = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

// Ambil daftar anak yang terhubung dengan akun orang tua ini
$anak_query = "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id_user_ortu = $id_ortu ORDER BY nama ASC";
$anak_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $anak_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Pilih anak aktif (default: anak pertama)
$id_santri_pilih = isset($_GET['id_santri']) ? (int) $_GET['id_santri'] : ($anak_list[0]['id'] ?? 0);
$santri = null;
foreach ($anak_list as $a) {
    if ($a['id'] == $id_santri_pilih) {
        $santri = $a;
        break;
    }
}

// Filter Bulan & Tahun (Default: Bulan & Tahun Saat Ini)
$bulan_pilih = isset($_GET['bulan']) ? str_pad((int)$_GET['bulan'], 2, '0', STR_PAD_LEFT) : date('m');
$tahun_pilih = isset($_GET['tahun']) ? (int) $_GET['tahun'] : (int) date('Y');
$periode     = "$tahun_pilih-$bulan_pilih";

$nama_bulan = [
    '01' => 'Januari', '02' => 'Februari', '03' => 'Maret', '04' => 'April',
    '05' => 'Mei', '06' => 'Juni', '07' => 'Juli', '08' => 'Agustus',
    '09' => 'September', '10' => 'Oktober', '11' => 'November', '12' => 'Desember'
];

$Nama_Hari_Indo = [
    'Sunday' => 'Minggu', 'Monday' => 'Senin', 'Tuesday' => 'Selasa',
    'Wednesday' => 'Rabu', 'Thursday' => 'Kamis', 'Friday' => 'Jumat', 'Saturday' => 'Sabtu'
];

$rekap = ['H' => 0, 'S' => 0, 'I' => 0, 'A' => 0];
$absensi_list = [];
$map_tanggal = []; // Untuk kalender visual

if ($santri) {
    $id_santri = (int) $santri['id'];

    // Ambil data absensi dari tabel `absensi` pada periode terpilih
    $q_absensi = "SELECT a.*, u.nama_lengkap as nama_ustadz 
                  FROM absensi a 
                  LEFT JOIN users u ON a.id_ustadz = u.id 
                  WHERE a.id_santri = $id_santri 
                    AND DATE_FORMAT(a.tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$periode'
                  ORDER BY a.tanggal DESC, a.id DESC";
    $res_absensi = mysqli_query($conn, $q_absensi);
    if ($res_absensi) {
        $absensi_list = mysqli_fetch_all($res_absensi, MYSQLI_ASSOC);
    }

    foreach ($absensi_list as $row) {
        $inisial = strtoupper(substr(trim($row['status']), 0, 1));
        if (isset($rekap[$inisial])) {
            $rekap[$inisial]++;
        }
        $tgl_angka = (int) date('j', strtotime($row['tanggal']));
        $map_tanggal[$tgl_angka] = [
            'inisial'    => $inisial,
            'keterangan' => $row['keterangan'] ?? '',
            'ustadz'     => $row['nama_ustadz'] ?? 'Ustadz/ah'
        ];
    }
}

$total_pertemuan = array_sum($rekap);
$persentase_hadir = $total_pertemuan > 0 ? round(($rekap['H'] / $total_pertemuan) * 100) : 0;

// Konfigurasi Kalender Bulan Terpilih
$jumlah_hari_bulan = cal_days_in_month(CAL_GREGORIAN, (int)$bulan_pilih, $tahun_pilih);
$hari_pertama_minggu = (int) date('N', strtotime("$tahun_pilih-$bulan_pilih-01")); // 1 (Senin) s/d 7 (Minggu)
?>
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="id">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <meta name="viewport" content="width=device-width, initial-scale=1.0">
    <title>Kehadiran Anak - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus</title>
    <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/lucide@latest"></script>
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@300;400;500;600;700;800&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    <style>body { font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif; }</style>
</head>
<body class="bg-slate-50 font-sans text-slate-800">

    <?php include 'includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

    <div class="ml-64 p-8">
        
        <!-- Header & Filter Periode -->
        <div class="flex flex-wrap justify-between items-end gap-4 mb-8">
            <div>
                <h1 class="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div class="p-2.5 bg-blue-100 rounded-xl">
                        <i data-lucide="calendar-check" class="w-7 h-7 text-blue-600"></i>
                    </div>
                    Kehadiran & Presensi Ananda
                </h1>
                <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-2">Pantau rekapitulasi kehadiran harian dan kedisiplinan ananda di Surau Tahfizh Firdaus.</p>
            </div>

            <!-- Filter Anak, Bulan & Tahun -->
            <form method="GET" action="absensi_anak.php" class="bg-white border border-slate-200 p-2.5 rounded-2xl shadow-sm flex flex-wrap items-center gap-2">
                <?php if (count($anak_list) > 1): ?>
                    <select name="id_santri" onchange="this.form.submit()" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 outline-none cursor-pointer">
                        <?php foreach ($anak_list as $a): ?>
                            <option value="<?= $a['id'] ?>" <?= $a['id'] == $id_santri_pilih ? 'selected' : '' ?>>
                                👦 <?= htmlspecialchars($a['nama']) ?>
                            </option>
                        <?php endforeach; ?>
                    </select>
                <?php endif; ?>

                <select name="bulan" onchange="this.form.submit()" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none cursor-pointer">
                    <?php foreach ($nama_bulan as $num => $nama_bln): ?>
                        <option value="<?= $num ?>" <?= $num == $bulan_pilih ? 'selected' : '' ?>><?= $nama_bln ?></option>
                    <?php endforeach; ?>
                </select>

                <select name="tahun" onchange="this.form.submit()" class="px-3 py-2 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 focus:ring-2 focus:ring-blue-500 outline-none cursor-pointer">
                    <?php for ($th = date('Y') - 2; $th <= date('Y') + 1; $th++): ?>
                        <option value="<?= $th ?>" <?= $th == $tahun_pilih ? 'selected' : '' ?>><?= $th ?></option>
                    <?php endfor; ?>
                </select>
            </form>
        </div>

        <?php if (!$santri): ?>
            <div class="bg-amber-50 border-l-4 border-amber-500 text-amber-800 p-6 rounded-2xl shadow-sm">
                <h3 class="font-bold text-base mb-1 flex items-center gap-2">
                    <i data-lucide="alert-triangle" class="w-5 h-5"></i> Data Anak Belum Terhubung
                </h3>
                <p class="text-xs">Akun orang tua Anda belum ditautkan dengan data santri. Silakan hubungi Ustadz/Ustadzah pembimbing.</p>
            </div>
        <?php else: ?>

        <!-- KARTU IDENTITAS & PERSENTASE KEHADIRAN -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-6 mb-8">
            
            <!-- Profil Ananda -->
            <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm flex items-center gap-4">
                <div class="w-16 h-16 rounded-2xl bg-gradient-to-br from-blue-600 to-indigo-600 text-white font-extrabold text-2xl flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 shadow-md shadow-blue-100">
                    <?= strtoupper(substr($santri['nama'], 0, 1)) ?>
                </div>
                <div class="min-w-0">
                    <span class="text-[10px] font-extrabold uppercase tracking-wider text-blue-600 bg-blue-50 px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-full border border-blue-200">
                        Periode <?= $nama_bulan[$bulan_pilih] ?> <?= $tahun_pilih ?>
                    </span>
                    <h2 class="text-xl font-extrabold text-slate-900 truncate mt-1.5"><?= htmlspecialchars($santri['nama']) ?></h2>
                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-500 mt-0.5">NIS: <?= htmlspecialchars($santri['nis'] ?: '-') ?> • <?= $santri['jenis_kelamin'] === 'L' ? 'Santriwan (Putra)' : 'Santriwati (Putri)' ?></p>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- Bar Persentase Kehadiran -->
            <div class="lg:col-span-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm flex flex-col justify-between">
                <div class="flex flex-wrap items-start justify-between gap-4 mb-3">
                    <div>
                        <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-400 uppercase tracking-wider">Tingkat Kehadiran Bulan Ini</p>
                        <div class="flex items-center gap-2 mt-1">
                            <h3 class="text-2xl font-black text-slate-900"><?= $persentase_hadir ?>%</h3>
                            <?php if ($total_pertemuan === 0): ?>
                                <span class="text-xs font-semibold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-slate-100 text-slate-500">Belum ada presensi</span>
                            <?php elseif ($persentase_hadir >= 85): ?>
                                <span class="text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700">✨ Sangat Disiplin</span>
                            <?php elseif ($persentase_hadir >= 70): ?>
                                <span class="text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-amber-100 text-amber-700">👍 Cukup Baik</span>
                            <?php else: ?>
                                <span class="text-xs font-bold px-2.5 py-0.5 rounded-lg bg-rose-100 text-rose-700">⚠️ Perlu Perhatian</span>
                            <?php endif; ?>
                        </div>
                    </div>
                    <div class="text-right">
                        <span class="text-xs font-semibold text-slate-500">Total Hari Tercatat:</span>
                        <p class="text-lg font-extrabold text-slate-800"><?= $total_pertemuan ?> Pertemuan</p>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <div class="w-full h-3.5 bg-slate-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-slate-200 mb-2">
                    <div class="h-full bg-gradient-to-r from-emerald-500 to-teal-500 rounded-full transition-all duration-500" style="width: <?= $persentase_hadir ?>%"></div>
                </div>

                <div class="flex justify-between text-xs text-slate-500">
                    <span>Hadir: <b class="text-emerald-600"><?= $rekap['H'] ?> Hari</b></span>
                    <span>Tidak Hadir (S/I/A): <b class="text-slate-700"><?= $rekap['S'] + $rekap['I'] + $rekap['A'] ?> Hari</b></span>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- 4 KARTU RINGKASAN STATUS -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-2 lg:grid-cols-4 gap-5 mb-8">
            <div class="bg-white border border-emerald-200 border-b-4 border-b-emerald-500 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <p class="text-xs font-bold text-emerald-600 uppercase tracking-wider">Hadir (H)</p>
                    <p class="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= $rekap['H'] ?></p>
                    <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Mengikuti halaqah</p>
                </div>
                <div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-emerald-50 text-emerald-600 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <i data-lucide="user-check" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="bg-white border border-blue-200 border-b-4 border-b-blue-500 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <p class="text-xs font-bold text-blue-600 uppercase tracking-wider">Sakit (S)</p>
                    <p class="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= $rekap['S'] ?></p>
                    <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Izin karena sakit</p>
                </div>
                <div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-blue-50 text-blue-600 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <i data-lucide="thermometer" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="bg-white border border-amber-200 border-b-4 border-b-amber-500 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <p class="text-xs font-bold text-amber-600 uppercase tracking-wider">Izin (I)</p>
                    <p class="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= $rekap['I'] ?></p>
                    <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Keperluan keluarga/sekolah</p>
                </div>
                <div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-amber-50 text-amber-600 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <i data-lucide="file-clock" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
                </div>
            </div>

            <div class="bg-white border border-rose-200 border-b-4 border-b-rose-500 rounded-2xl p-5 shadow-sm flex items-center justify-between">
                <div>
                    <p class="text-xs font-bold text-rose-600 uppercase tracking-wider">Alfa (A)</p>
                    <p class="text-3xl font-black text-slate-900 mt-1"><?= $rekap['A'] ?></p>
                    <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Tanpa keterangan</p>
                </div>
                <div class="w-11 h-11 rounded-2xl bg-rose-50 text-rose-600 flex items-center justify-center">
                    <i data-lucide="user-x" class="w-6 h-6"></i>
                </div>
            </div>
        </div>

        <!-- GRID KALENDER VISUAL & TABEL LOG RIWAYAT -->
        <div class="grid grid-cols-1 lg:grid-cols-3 gap-8">
            
            <!-- KALENDER VISUAL BULANAN -->
            <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl p-6 shadow-sm h-fit">
                <div class="flex items-center justify-between mb-4">
                    <div>
                        <h3 class="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">
                            <i data-lucide="calendar-days" class="w-5 h-5 text-blue-600"></i>
                            Kalender <?= $nama_bulan[$bulan_pilih] ?> <?= $tahun_pilih ?>
                        </h3>
                        <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-0.5">Peta visual kehadiran harian</p>
                    </div>
                </div>

                <!-- Header Nama Hari -->
                <div class="grid grid-cols-7 gap-1.5 text-center text-[10px] font-bold uppercase text-slate-400 mb-2">
                    <div>Sen</div><div>Sel</div><div>Rab</div><div>Kam</div><div>Jum</div><div>Sab</div><div class="text-rose-400">Min</div>
                </div>

                <!-- Grid Tanggal -->
                <div class="grid grid-cols-7 gap-1.5 text-xs">
                    <?php
                    // Kotak kosong sebelum tanggal 1
                    for ($kosong = 1; $kosong < $hari_pertama_minggu; $kosong++) {
                        echo '<div class="h-10 rounded-lg bg-slate-50/50"></div>';
                    }

                    // Loop Tanggal 1 s/d akhir bulan
                    for ($tgl = 1; $tgl <= $jumlah_hari_bulan; $tgl++) {
                        $info = $map_tanggal[$tgl] ?? null;
                        $cls_box = 'bg-slate-50 text-slate-600 border-slate-200';
                        $title_tip = "Tanggal $tgl: Belum ada catatan";

                        if ($info) {
                            $st = $info['inisial'];
                            if ($st === 'H') {
                                $cls_box = 'bg-emerald-500 text-white border-emerald-600 font-bold shadow-sm';
                                $title_tip = "Tgl $tgl: HADIR";
                            } elseif ($st === 'S') {
                                $cls_box = 'bg-blue-500 text-white border-blue-600 font-bold shadow-sm';
                                $title_tip = "Tgl $tgl: SAKIT (" . ($info['keterangan'] ?: '-') . ")";
                            } elseif ($st === 'I') {
                                $cls_box = 'bg-amber-500 text-white border-amber-600 font-bold shadow-sm';
                                $title_tip = "Tgl $tgl: IZIN (" . ($info['keterangan'] ?: '-') . ")";
                            } elseif ($st === 'A') {
                                $cls_box = 'bg-rose-500 text-white border-rose-600 font-bold shadow-sm';
                                $title_tip = "Tgl $tgl: ALFA";
                            }
                        }

                        echo '<div title="' . htmlspecialchars($title_tip) . '" class="h-10 rounded-xl border flex flex-col items-center justify-center cursor-default transition ' . $cls_box . '">';
                        echo '<span>' . $tgl . '</span>';
                        if ($info) {
                            echo '<span class="text-[9px] leading-none opacity-90">' . $info['inisial'] . '</span>';
                        }
                        echo '</div>';
                    }
                    ?>
                </div>

                <!-- Legenda Warna -->
                <div class="mt-5 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 grid grid-cols-2 gap-2 text-[11px] text-slate-600">
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2"><span class="w-3 h-3 rounded bg-emerald-500 inline-block"></span> Hadir (H)</div>
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2"><span class="w-3 h-3 rounded bg-blue-500 inline-block"></span> Sakit (S)</div>
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2"><span class="w-3 h-3 rounded bg-amber-500 inline-block"></span> Izin (I)</div>
                    <div class="flex items-center gap-2"><span class="w-3 h-3 rounded bg-rose-500 inline-block"></span> Alfa (A)</div>
                </div>
            </div>

            <!-- TABEL RINCIAN PRESENSI -->
            <div class="lg:col-span-2 bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm overflow-hidden">
                <div class="p-6 border-b border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/50 flex flex-wrap justify-between items-center gap-4">
                    <div>
                        <h3 class="text-base font-bold text-slate-800 flex items-center gap-2">
                            <i data-lucide="list-checks" class="w-5 h-5 text-blue-600"></i> Rincian Log Kehadiran
                        </h3>
                        <p class="text-xs text-slate-500">Daftar presensi tercatat pada bulan <?= $nama_bulan[$bulan_pilih] ?> <?= $tahun_pilih ?></p>
                    </div>

                    <select id="filterStatus" onchange="filterLogAbsensi()" class="px-3 py-1.5 bg-white border border-slate-300 rounded-xl text-xs font-semibold text-slate-700 outline-none cursor-pointer">
                        <option value="ALL">Semua Status</option>
                        <option value="H">Hadir Saja</option>
                        <option value="S">Sakit Saja</option>
                        <option value="I">Izin Saja</option>
                        <option value="A">Alfa Saja</option>
                    </select>
                </div>

                <div class="overflow-x-auto">
                    <table class="w-full text-left border-collapse">
                        <thead class="bg-white text-slate-400 uppercase text-[11px] tracking-wider border-b border-slate-200">
                            <tr>
                                <th class="py-3.5 px-6 font-semibold">Hari & Tanggal</th>
                                <th class="py-3.5 px-4 font-semibold text-center">Status</th>
                                <th class="py-3.5 px-4 font-semibold">Keterangan</th>
                                <th class="py-3.5 px-6 font-semibold">Dicatat Oleh</th>
                            </tr>
                        </thead>
                        <tbody class="text-sm divide-y divide-slate-100">
                            <?php if (empty($absensi_list)): ?>
                                <tr>
                                    <td colspan="4" class="py-12 text-center">
                                        <div class="flex flex-col items-center justify-center text-slate-400">
                                            <i data-lucide="calendar-off" class="w-10 h-10 mb-2 opacity-40"></i>
                                            <p class="font-medium text-slate-600">Belum ada catatan kehadiran di bulan ini</p>
                                            <p class="text-xs mt-1">Pilih bulan lain pada filter di kanan atas untuk melihat riwayat sebelumnya.</p>
                                        </div>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                            <?php else: ?>
                                <?php foreach ($absensi_list as $a): 
                                    $in = strtoupper(substr(trim($a['status']), 0, 1));
                                    if ($in === 'H') {
                                        $badge = 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700 border-emerald-200';
                                        $label = 'Hadir';
                                    } elseif ($in === 'S') {
                                        $badge = 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700 border-blue-200';
                                        $label = 'Sakit';
                                    } elseif ($in === 'I') {
                                        $badge = 'bg-amber-100 text-amber-700 border-amber-200';
                                        $label = 'Izin';
                                    } else {
                                        $badge = 'bg-rose-100 text-rose-700 border-rose-200';
                                        $label = 'Alfa';
                                    }

                                    $nama_hari_en = date('l', strtotime($a['tanggal']));
                                    $hari_indo    = $Nama_Hari_Indo[$nama_hari_en] ?? $nama_hari_en;
                                ?>
                                <tr class="baris-absensi hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition" data-status="<?= $in ?>">
                                    <td class="py-4 px-6">
                                        <p class="font-bold text-slate-800"><?= $hari_indo ?>, <?= date('d M Y', strtotime($a['tanggal'])) ?></p>
                                    </td>
                                    <td class="py-4 px-4 text-center">
                                        <span class="inline-flex items-center px-3 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold border <?= $badge ?>">
                                            <?= $label ?>
                                        </span>
                                    </td>
                                    <td class="py-4 px-4 text-xs text-slate-600">
                                        <?= !empty($a['keterangan']) ? htmlspecialchars($a['keterangan']) : '<span class="text-slate-400 italic">Hadir tepat waktu / Tanpa catatan</span>' ?>
                                    </td>
                                    <td class="py-4 px-6 text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">
                                        <?= htmlspecialchars($a['nama_ustadz'] ?? 'Ustadz Pembimbing') ?>
                                    </td>
                                </tr>
                                <?php endforeach; ?>
                            <?php endif; ?>
                        </tbody>
                    </table>
                </div>
            </div>

        </div>

        <?php endif; ?>
    </div>

    <script>
        lucide.createIcons();

        function filterLogAbsensi() {
            const st = document.getElementById('filterStatus').value;
            document.querySelectorAll('.baris-absensi').forEach(tr => {
                tr.style.display = (st === 'ALL' || tr.dataset.status === st) ? '' : 'none';
            });
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

<?php
$host     = "localhost";
$username = "root";       
$password = "";  
$database = "hafalan_santri";

$conn = new mysqli($host, $username, $password, $database);

if ($conn->connect_error) {
    die("Koneksi database gagal: " . $conn->connect_error);
}

$conn->set_charset("utf8mb4");

?>


================================================================================
FILE: C:\xampp\htdocs\SIRAH\dashboard.php
PATH: C:\xampp\htdocs\SIRAH\dashboard.php
================================================================================
<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$nama = $_SESSION['nama_lengkap'];
$role = $_SESSION['role'];

$chart_labels = [];
$chart_ziyadah = [];
$chart_murajaah = [];
$stat = ['total_ayat' => 0, 'total_murajaah' => 0, 'total_tasmi' => 0, 'santri_aktif' => 0];
$total_santri = 0;
$total_ustadz = 0;
$predikat_data = ['A' => 0, 'B' => 0, 'C' => 0];
$aktivitas_list = [];
$target_widget = ['total' => 0, 'tercapai' => 0, 'persentase' => 0, 'label' => ''];

if (in_array($role, ['ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    $total_santri = (int) mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) as total FROM santri"))['total'];
    $total_ustadz = (int) mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COUNT(*) as total FROM users WHERE role = 'ustadz'"))['total'];

    $bulan_ini = date('Y-m');
    $stat_query = "SELECT 
                    COUNT(DISTINCT id_santri) as santri_aktif,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_ayat,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_murajaah,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'tasmi' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_tasmi
                  FROM hafalan 
                  WHERE DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan_ini'";
    $stat = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $stat_query));

    for ($i = 5; $i >= 0; $i--) {
        $bulan = date('Y-m', strtotime("-$i months"));
        $chart_labels[] = date('M Y', strtotime("-$i months"));
        $q = "SELECT 
                COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as z,
                COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as m
              FROM hafalan WHERE DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan'";
        $d = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $q));
        $chart_ziyadah[] = (int) $d['z'];
        $chart_murajaah[] = (int) $d['m'];
    }

    $predikat_res = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT predikat, COUNT(*) as jumlah FROM hafalan WHERE DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan_ini' GROUP BY predikat");
    while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($predikat_res)) {
        if (isset($predikat_data[$row['predikat']])) {
            $predikat_data[$row['predikat']] = (int) $row['jumlah'];
        }
    }

    $target_widget['label'] = $role === 'pimpinan' ? 'Santri Mencapai Target Bulanan' : 'Realisasi Target Santri Binaan';
    $target_widget['total'] = $total_santri;
    $capaian_query = "SELECT COUNT(DISTINCT s.id) as tercapai
                      FROM santri s
                      JOIN target_hafalan t ON s.id = t.id_santri
                      LEFT JOIN (
                          SELECT id_santri, SUM(jumlah_ayat) as total_ayat
                          FROM hafalan 
                          WHERE jenis = 'ziyadah' AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan_ini'
                          GROUP BY id_santri
                      ) h ON s.id = h.id_santri
                      WHERE (t.target_ayat_per_hari * 20) <= COALESCE(h.total_ayat, 0)";
    $target_widget['tercapai'] = (int) mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $capaian_query))['tercapai'];
    $target_widget['persentase'] = $target_widget['total'] > 0 ? round(($target_widget['tercapai'] / $target_widget['total']) * 100) : 0;

    $aktivitas_query = "SELECT h.*, s.nama as nama_santri, u.nama_lengkap as nama_ustadz
                        FROM hafalan h
                        JOIN santri s ON h.id_santri = s.id
                        LEFT JOIN users u ON h.id_ustadz = u.id
                        ORDER BY h.created_at DESC LIMIT 6";
    $aktivitas_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $aktivitas_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

} elseif ($role === 'ortu') {
    $id_ortu = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
    $santri = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id_user_ortu = $id_ortu LIMIT 1"));

    if ($santri) {
        $id_santri = $santri['id'];
        $bulan_ini = date('Y-m');

        $stat_query = "SELECT 
                        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_ayat,
                        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_murajaah,
                        COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'tasmi' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_tasmi
                      FROM hafalan 
                      WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan_ini'";
        $stat = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $stat_query));

        for ($i = 5; $i >= 0; $i--) {
            $bulan = date('Y-m', strtotime("-$i months"));
            $chart_labels[] = date('M Y', strtotime("-$i months"));
            $q = "SELECT 
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as z,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as m
                  FROM hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan'";
            $d = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $q));
            $chart_ziyadah[] = (int) $d['z'];
            $chart_murajaah[] = (int) $d['m'];
        }

        $target_q = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT target_ayat_per_hari FROM target_hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1");
        $target_anak = ($target_q && mysqli_num_rows($target_q) > 0) ? mysqli_fetch_assoc($target_q) : null;
        $target_ayat_harian = $target_anak ? (int) $target_anak['target_ayat_per_hari'] : 0;
        
        $target_widget['label'] = 'Capaian Target Bulanan Ananda';
        $target_widget['total'] = $target_ayat_harian * 20;
        $target_widget['tercapai'] = (int) $stat['total_ayat'];
        $target_widget['persentase'] = $target_widget['total'] > 0 ? min(100, round(($target_widget['tercapai'] / $target_widget['total']) * 100)) : 0;

        $aktivitas_query = "SELECT h.*, u.nama_lengkap as nama_ustadz
                            FROM hafalan h
                            LEFT JOIN users u ON h.id_ustadz = u.id
                            WHERE h.id_santri = $id_santri
                            ORDER BY h.created_at DESC LIMIT 6";
        $aktivitas_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $aktivitas_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
    }
}

                        <a href="rapor_santri.php" class="bg-white/10 hover:bg-white/20 border border-white/20 text-white text-xs font-semibold px-4 py-2.5 rounded-xl transition flex items-center gap-2">

                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-400 mt-1">Sesi ujian bulan ini</p>
                </div>

                    <h2 class="text-base font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-2">

                            </p>

                        <div class="bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-500 to-teal-500 h-full rounded-full transition-all duration-500" style="width: <?= $target_widget['persentase'] ?>%"></div>

                    <div class="flex justify-between text-xs font-medium text-slate-500">

                    </div>

                <div class="mt-6 pt-4 border-t border-slate-100 flex items-center justify-between text-xs">

                        Belum ada aktivitas setoran yang tercatat.
                    </div>
                <?php else: ?>
                    <div class="space-y-3">
                        <?php foreach ($aktivitas_list as $akt): 
                            $badge_jenis = $akt['jenis'] == 'ziyadah' ? 'bg-emerald-100 text-emerald-700' : ($akt['jenis'] == 'murajaah' ? 'bg-blue-100 text-blue-700' : 'bg-purple-100 text-purple-700');
                        ?>
                            <div class="flex items-center justify-between p-3 rounded-xl bg-slate-50 border border-slate-100">
                                <div class="flex items-center gap-3 min-w-0">
                                    <span class="px-2 py-1 rounded-lg text-[10px] font-bold uppercase <?= $badge_jenis ?>"><?= $akt['jenis'] ?></span>
                                    <div class="truncate">
                                        <p class="text-xs font-bold text-slate-800 truncate">
                                            <?= $role === 'ortu' ? htmlspecialchars($akt['surat']) : htmlspecialchars($akt['nama_santri']) . ' • ' . htmlspecialchars($akt['surat']) ?>
                                        </p>
                                        <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">
                                            Ayat <?= $akt['ayat_awal'] ?>-<?= $akt['ayat_akhir'] ?> • <?= date('d M H:i', strtotime($akt['created_at'])) ?>
                                        </p>
                                    </div>
                                </div>
                                <span class="w-7 h-7 rounded-lg font-bold text-xs flex items-center justify-center shrink-0 <?= $akt['predikat'] == 'A' ? 'bg-emerald-500 text-white' : ($akt['predikat'] == 'B' ? 'bg-amber-500 text-white' : 'bg-rose-500 text-white') ?>">
                                    <?= $akt['predikat'] ?>
                                </span>
                            </div>
                        <?php endforeach; ?>
                    </div>
                <?php endif; ?>
            </div>

        </div>

    </div>

    <script>
        lucide.createIcons();

        const ctxTren = document.getElementById('progresChart').getContext('2d');
        new Chart(ctxTren, {
            type: 'line',
            data: {
                labels: <?= json_encode($chart_labels) ?>,
                datasets: [
                    {
                        label: 'Ziyadah (Ayat)',
                        data: <?= json_encode($chart_ziyadah) ?>,
                        borderColor: '#10b981',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(16, 185, 129, 0.12)',
                        borderWidth: 2.5,
                        tension: 0.4,
                        fill: true,
                        pointRadius: 4,
                        pointBackgroundColor: '#10b981'
                    },
                    {
                        label: "Muraja'ah (Kali)",
                        data: <?= json_encode($chart_murajaah) ?>,
                        borderColor: '#3b82f6',
                        backgroundColor: 'rgba(59, 130, 246, 0.08)',
                        borderWidth: 2.5,
                        tension: 0.4,
                        fill: true,
                        pointRadius: 4,
                        pointBackgroundColor: '#3b82f6'
                    }
                ]
            },
            options: {
                responsive: true,
                maintainAspectRatio: false,
                plugins: { legend: { display: false } },
                scales: {
                    y: { beginAtZero: true, grid: { color: '#f1f5f9' } },
                    x: { grid: { display: false } }
                }
            }
        });

        <?php if (in_array($role, ['ustadz', 'pimpinan'])): ?>
        const ctxDonat = document.getElementById('predikatChart').getContext('2d');

$nama = $_SESSION['nama_lengkap'] ?? 'Pengguna';

// Penyesuaian label role & warna badge
$label_role = 'Pengguna';
$badge_role_class = 'bg-slate-800 text-slate-300 border-slate-700';

if ($role === 'ustadz') {
    if (stripos($nama, 'ustadzah') !== false) {
        $label_role = 'Ustadzah';
        $badge_role_class = 'bg-teal-500/15 text-teal-300 border-teal-500/30';
    } else {
        $label_role = 'Ustadz';
        $badge_role_class = 'bg-indigo-500/15 text-indigo-300 border-indigo-500/30';
    }
} elseif ($role === 'ortu') {
    $label_role = 'Wali Santri';
    $badge_role_class = 'bg-emerald-500/15 text-emerald-300 border-emerald-500/30';
} elseif ($role === 'pimpinan') {
    $label_role = 'Pimpinan';
    $badge_role_class = 'bg-amber-500/15 text-amber-300 border-amber-500/30';
}

// Helper class untuk item menu
function navItemClass($target_files, $current_file) {
    $targets = (array) $target_files;
    $is_active = in_array($current_file, $targets);
    if ($is_active) {
        return 'relative bg-gradient-to-r from-indigo-600/90 to-indigo-600/70 text-white font-semibold shadow-md shadow-indigo-950/50 border border-indigo-400/20';
    }
    return 'text-slate-400 hover:bg-slate-800/70 hover:text-slate-100 border border-transparent';
}

function isNavActive($target_files, $current_file) {
    return in_array($current_file, (array) $target_files);
}

<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

// Keamanan: Hanya Ustadz / Pimpinan
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$id_ustadz = $_SESSION['user_id'];

// Proses simpan absensi
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $tanggal = $_POST['tanggal'];
    
    foreach ($_POST['status'] as $id_santri => $status) {
        $keterangan = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['keterangan'][$id_santri] ?? '');
        
        // Cek apakah sudah ada absensi untuk santri ini di tanggal tersebut
        $cek = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM absensi WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND tanggal = '$tanggal'");
        
        if (mysqli_num_rows($cek) > 0) {
            // Update jika sudah ada
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE absensi SET status = '$status', keterangan = '$keterangan', id_ustadz = $id_ustadz WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND tanggal = '$tanggal'");
        } else {
            // Insert jika belum ada
            mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO absensi (id_santri, tanggal, status, keterangan, id_ustadz) VALUES ($id_santri, '$tanggal', '$status', '$keterangan', $id_ustadz)");
        }
    }
    
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data absensi tanggal " . date('d F Y', strtotime($tanggal)) . " berhasil disimpan!";
    header("Location: input_kehadiran.php?tanggal=$tanggal");
    exit;
}

// Ambil tanggal (default hari ini)
$tanggal = $_GET['tanggal'] ?? date('Y-m-d');

// Ambil SEMUA santri
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nama, nis FROM santri ORDER BY nama ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Ambil data absensi yang sudah diinput untuk tanggal tersebut
$absensi_hari_ini = [];
$statistik = ['H' => 0, 'S' => 0, 'I' => 0, 'A' => 0, 'Belum' => count($santri_list)];

if (!empty($santri_list)) {
    $absensi_result = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM absensi WHERE tanggal = '$tanggal'");
    while ($row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($absensi_result)) {
        $absensi_hari_ini[$row['id_santri']] = $row;
        // Hitung statistik (Status di db Anda sepertinya 'H','S','I','A' atau 'Hadir','Sakit', dsb. Kita asumsikan huruf depan)
        $inisial = strtoupper(substr($row['status'], 0, 1)); 
        if (isset($statistik[$inisial])) {
            $statistik[$inisial]++;
            $statistik['Belum']--;
        }
    }
}
?>

        /* Hilangkan radio circle asli */
        .status-radio input[type="radio"] { display: none; }
        
        /* Desain label radio ketika TERPILIH */
        .status-radio input[value^="H"]:checked + div { background-color: #10b981; color: white; border-color: #10b981; } /* Emerald untuk Hadir */
        .status-radio input[value^="S"]:checked + div { background-color: #3b82f6; color: white; border-color: #3b82f6; } /* Blue untuk Sakit */
        .status-radio input[value^="I"]:checked + div { background-color: #f59e0b; color: white; border-color: #f59e0b; } /* Amber untuk Izin */
        .status-radio input[value^="A"]:checked + div { background-color: #f43f5e; color: white; border-color: #f43f5e; } /* Rose untuk Alfa */
        
        /* Desain hover agar terlihat bisa diklik */
        .status-radio div:hover { filter: brightness(0.95); }
    </style>

            <p class="text-2xl font-black text-rose-600"><?= $statistik['A'] ?></p>
        </div>

    <!-- Form Absensi Massal -->
    <form method="POST" class="bg-white border border-slate-200 rounded-2xl shadow-sm overflow-hidden mb-10">
        <input type="hidden" name="tanggal" value="<?= $tanggal ?>">
        
        <div class="px-6 py-4 border-b border-slate-100 bg-slate-50/50 flex justify-between items-center">
            <h3 class="text-base font-semibold text-slate-800 flex items-center gap-2">
                <i data-lucide="users" class="w-4 h-4 text-indigo-600"></i> Daftar Presensi Santri
            </h3>
            <!-- Tombol Simpan Atas (jika data banyak) -->
            <button type="submit" class="bg-indigo-600 hover:bg-indigo-700 text-white font-semibold py-1.5 px-4 rounded-lg transition shadow flex items-center gap-2 text-xs">
                <i data-lucide="save" class="w-3 h-3"></i> Simpan
            </button>
        </div>

        <div class="overflow-x-auto">
            <table class="w-full text-left">
                <thead class="bg-white text-slate-400 text-[11px] uppercase tracking-wider border-b border-slate-200">

                        <th class="py-4 px-6 font-semibold w-10 text-center">No</th>
                        <th class="py-4 px-4 font-semibold">Profil Santri</th>
                        <th class="py-4 px-4 font-semibold text-center w-[340px]">Status Kehadiran</th>
                        <th class="py-4 px-6 font-semibold">Keterangan Opsional</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody class="text-sm divide-y divide-slate-100">
                    <?php 
                    // Set status DB (sesuaikan dgn ENUM db anda: 'Hadir','Sakit','Izin','Alfa' atau 'H','S','I','A')
                    // Default nilai form kita gunakan lengkap
                    $opsi_status = ['Hadir', 'Sakit', 'Izin', 'Alfa']; 

                    foreach ($santri_list as $index => $s): 
                        // Jika belum ada data di DB, default ke Hadir
                        $status_aktif = $absensi_hari_ini[$s['id']]['status'] ?? 'Hadir';
                        
                        // Perbaikan jika format DB hanya 'H', konversi ke 'Hadir' untuk UI form
                        if ($status_aktif == 'H') $status_aktif = 'Hadir';
                        if ($status_aktif == 'S') $status_aktif = 'Sakit';
                        if ($status_aktif == 'I') $status_aktif = 'Izin';
                        if ($status_aktif == 'A') $status_aktif = 'Alfa';

                        $keterangan_aktif = $absensi_hari_ini[$s['id']]['keterangan'] ?? '';
                    ?>
                    <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50 transition group">
                        <td class="py-3 px-6 text-slate-400 font-medium text-center"><?= $index + 1 ?></td>
                        <td class="py-3 px-4">
                            <p class="font-bold text-slate-800"><?= htmlspecialchars($s['nama']) ?></p>
                            <p class="text-xs text-slate-400">NIS: <?= htmlspecialchars($s['nis'] ?? '-') ?></p>
                        </td>

PATH: C:\xampp\htdocs\SIRAH\input_pr.php

<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

// Keamanan: Hanya Ustadz atau Pimpinan
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) { 
    header("Location: login.php"); 
    exit; 
}

// Otomatis tambahkan kolom status_ortu di tabel pr jika belum ada
$cek_kolom_pr = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM pr LIKE 'status_ortu'");
if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_kolom_pr) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE pr ADD COLUMN status_ortu ENUM('Belum','Selesai') DEFAULT 'Belum' AFTER deskripsi");
}

$id_ustadz = $_SESSION['user_id'];
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nama, nis FROM santri ORDER BY nama ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {   
    $tanggal = $_POST['tanggal'];
    $judul = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['judul']));
    $deskripsi = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['deskripsi']));
    
    // Fitur Baru: Bisa berikan PR ke semua santri sekaligus
    if ($_POST['id_santri'] === 'semua') {
        foreach ($santri_list as $s) {
            $id_s = $s['id'];
            mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO pr (id_santri, tanggal, judul, deskripsi, id_ustadz) VALUES ($id_s, '$tanggal', '$judul', '$deskripsi', $id_ustadz)");
        }
        $_SESSION['success'] = "PR berhasil diberikan kepada SEMUA santri!";
    } else {
        $id_santri = (int) $_POST['id_santri'];
        mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO pr (id_santri, tanggal, judul, deskripsi, id_ustadz) VALUES ($id_santri, '$tanggal', '$judul', '$deskripsi', $id_ustadz)");
        $_SESSION['success'] = "PR berhasil diberikan kepada santri yang dipilih!";
    }
    
    header("Location: input_pr.php"); 
    exit;
}

// Ambil riwayat PR yang diberikan hari ini
$riwayat_query = "SELECT p.*, s.nama as nama_santri FROM pr p JOIN santri s ON p.id_santri = s.id WHERE p.tanggal = CURDATE() ORDER BY p.id DESC";
$riwayat_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $riwayat_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
?>
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="id">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <title>Berikan PR - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus</title>
    <script src="https://cdn.tailwindcss.com"></script>
    <script src="https://unpkg.com/lucide@latest"></script>
    <link href="https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@300;400;500;600;700&display=swap" rel="stylesheet">
    <style>body { font-family: 'Inter', sans-serif; }</style>
</head>
<body class="bg-slate-50 font-sans text-slate-800">

    <?php include 'includes/sidebar.php'; ?>

    <div class="ml-64 p-8">
        
        <!-- Header Halaman -->
        <div class="flex justify-between items-end mb-8">
            <div>
                <h1 class="text-3xl font-bold text-slate-900 flex items-center gap-3">
                    <div class="p-2 bg-orange-100 rounded-lg"><i data-lucide="clipboard-list" class="w-6 h-6 text-orange-600"></i></div>
                    Berikan Tugas Rumah (PR)
                </h1>
                <p class="text-sm text-slate-500 mt-2">Berikan target hafalan atau muraja'ah mandiri di rumah untuk santri.</p>
            </div>
            <div class="bg-white border border-slate-200 px-5 py-2.5 rounded-xl shadow-sm text-sm font-medium text-slate-700 flex items-center gap-2">
                <i data-lucide="calendar" class="w-4 h-4 text-orange-500"></i> <?= date('d F Y') ?>
            </div>
        </div>

        <?php if (isset($_SESSION['success'])): ?>

    header("Location: login.php");

$id_santri    = isset($_GET['id_santri']) ? (int) $_GET['id_santri'] : ($santri_list[0]['id'] ?? 0);
$juz          = isset($_GET['juz']) ? (int) $_GET['juz'] : 1;
$semester     = isset($_GET['semester']) ? $_GET['semester'] : '2 (Dua)';
$tahun_ajaran = isset($_GET['tahun_ajaran']) ? $_GET['tahun_ajaran'] : date('Y');

$nilai_data = null;
$santri_aktif = null;

if ($id_santri > 0) {
    foreach ($santri_list as $s) {
        if ($s['id'] == $id_santri) {
            $santri_aktif = $s;
            break;
        }
    }
}

                        <input type="number" name="nilai[]" value="'.htmlspecialchars((string)$nilai).'" min="0" max="100" placeholder="Nilai" class="w-full px-3 py-1.5 border border-slate-300 rounded text-sm font-semibold text-center focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none">

            function hapusPojok(btn) {
                btn.closest('.pojok-item').remove();
                updateNomorPojok();
            }

            function tambahPojok() {
                const container = document.getElementById('pojok-container');
                const div = document.createElement('div');
                div.className = 'pojok-item p-3 bg-slate-50 border border-slate-200 rounded-lg relative group';
                div.innerHTML = `
                    <button type="button" onclick="hapusPojok(this)" class="absolute top-2 right-2 text-slate-400 hover:text-red-500 opacity-0 group-hover:opacity-100 transition"><i data-lucide="x-circle" class="w-5 h-5"></i></button>
                    <label class="block text-xs font-bold text-slate-700 mb-1"><span class="nomor-pojok"></span></label>
                    <input type="text" name="materi[]" class="w-full px-2 py-1.5 mb-2 border border-slate-300 rounded text-xs text-slate-700 focus:ring-1 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none" placeholder="Nama Materi" required>
                    <input type="number" name="nilai[]" min="0" max="100" placeholder="Nilai" class="w-full px-3 py-1.5 border border-slate-300 rounded text-sm font-semibold text-center focus:ring-2 focus:ring-indigo-500 outline-none">
                `;
                container.appendChild(div);
                updateNomorPojok();
                lucide.createIcons();
            }

            function updateNomorPojok() {
                const items = document.querySelectorAll('.nomor-pojok');
                items.forEach((item, index) => {
                    item.innerText = index + 1;
                });
            }
        </script>

<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$santri_query = "SELECT id, nama, nis FROM santri ORDER BY nama ASC";
$santri_result = mysqli_query($conn, $santri_query);
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all($santri_result, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

$jenis_halaman = 'tasmi'; 
$riwayat_query = "SELECT h.*, s.nama as nama_santri FROM hafalan h JOIN santri s ON h.id_santri = s.id WHERE h.tanggal = CURDATE() AND h.jenis = '$jenis_halaman' ORDER BY h.id DESC";
$riwayat_result = mysqli_query($conn, $riwayat_query);
$riwayat_list = mysqli_fetch_all($riwayat_result, MYSQLI_ASSOC);
?>
<!DOCTYPE html>
<html lang="id">
<head>
    <meta charset="UTF-8">
    <title>Input Tasmi' - Surau Tahfizh Firdaus</title>

    <script src="https://unpkg.com/lucide@latest"></script>

        /* Sembunyikan radio circle bawaan */

        /* Style saat radio card dipilih */
        .radio-card input[type="radio"]:checked + div {
            border-color: currentColor;
            background-color: currentColor;
            color: white;
            box-shadow: 0 4px 6px -1px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1), 0 2px 4px -1px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.06);
        }
        .radio-card input[type="radio"]:checked + div .text-subtitle { color: rgba(255,255,255,0.8); }

<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

// 🔒 Keamanan: Ustadz & Pimpinan dapat mengakses
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: dashboard.php");
    exit;
}

$query = "SELECT santri.*, users.username as username_ortu 
          FROM santri 
          LEFT JOIN users ON santri.id_user_ortu = users.id 
          ORDER BY santri.nama ASC";
$result = mysqli_query($conn, $query);
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all($result, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Hitung Statistik Santri
$total_santri = count($santri_list);
$total_putra  = 0;
$total_putri  = 0;
foreach ($santri_list as $s) {
    if ($s['jenis_kelamin'] === 'L') $total_putra++;
    if ($s['jenis_kelamin'] === 'P') $total_putri++;
}

                            <?php foreach ($santri_list as $i => $s):

    <script>
        lucide.createIcons();

        // Buka / Tutup Form Tambah Santri
        function toggleFormSantri() {
            const form = document.getElementById('formTambahContainer');
            form.classList.toggle('hidden');
            if (!form.classList.contains('hidden')) {
                document.getElementById('inputNamaSantri').focus();
            }
        }

        // Otomatis sarankan username ortu dari kata pertama nama santri
        function generateUsernameOrtu(nama) {
            const inputUser = document.getElementById('inputUsernameOrtu');
            const bersih = nama.trim().toLowerCase().replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, '');
            const kataPertama = bersih.split(/\s+/)[0] || '';
            inputUser.value = kataPertama ? 'ortu_' + kataPertama : '';
        }

        // Filter Live Search & Jenis Kelamin
        function filterTabelSantri() {
            const keyword = document.getElementById('cariSantri').value.toLowerCase();
            const jk = document.getElementById('filterJK').value;
            const baris = document.querySelectorAll('.baris-santri');
            let tampil = 0;

            baris.forEach((tr) => {
                const dataSearch = tr.getAttribute('data-search');
                const dataJk = tr.getAttribute('data-jk');
                
                const cocokKata = dataSearch.includes(keyword);
                const cocokJk = (jk === 'ALL' || dataJk === jk);

                if (cocokKata && cocokJk) {
                    tr.style.display = '';
                    tampil++;
                    tr.querySelector('.nomor-urut').innerText = tampil;
                } else {
                    tr.style.display = 'none';
                }
            });

            document.getElementById('jumlahTampil').innerText = tampil;
        }

        function bukaModalEdit(data) {
            document.getElementById('edit_id_santri').value = data.id;
            document.getElementById('edit_id_user_ortu').value = data.id_user_ortu || 0;
            document.getElementById('edit_nama').value = data.nama || '';
            document.getElementById('edit_nis').value = data.nis || '';
            document.getElementById('edit_jk').value = data.jenis_kelamin || 'L';
            document.getElementById('edit_tgl_lahir').value = data.tanggal_lahir || '';
            document.getElementById('edit_alamat').value = data.alamat || '';
            document.getElementById('edit_nama_ortu').value = data.nama_ortu || '';
            document.getElementById('edit_no_hp').value = data.no_hp_ortu || '';
            document.getElementById('edit_username_ortu').value = data.username_ortu || '';
            
            document.getElementById('modalEditSantri').classList.remove('hidden');
            lucide.createIcons();
        }

        function tutupModalEdit() {
            document.getElementById('modalEditSantri').classList.add('hidden');
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

// 🔒 Keamanan: Hanya Pimpinan
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || $_SESSION['role'] !== 'pimpinan') {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

// Otomatis tambahkan kolom jenis_kelamin di tabel users jika belum ada
$cek_kolom = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM users LIKE 'jenis_kelamin'");
if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_kolom) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN jenis_kelamin ENUM('L','P') DEFAULT 'L' AFTER nama_lengkap");
}

// Otomatis tambahkan kolom id_halaqah di tabel users jika belum ada
$cek_kolom_h = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM users LIKE 'id_halaqah'");
if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_kolom_h) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE users ADD COLUMN id_halaqah INT(11) DEFAULT NULL AFTER role");
}

// === 1. PROSES TAMBAH USTADZ / USTADZAH ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['tambah_ustadz'])) {
    $nama_lengkap  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_lengkap']));
    $jenis_kelamin = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
    $username      = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['username']));
    $password      = password_hash($_POST['password'], PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
    $nama_lokal    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_lokal']));
    $sebutan       = ($jenis_kelamin === 'P') ? 'Ustadzah' : 'Ustadz';

    // Cek apakah Lokal/Halaqah sudah ada, jika belum buat baru
    $cek_lokal = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM halaqah WHERE nama_halaqah = '$nama_lokal'");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_lokal) > 0) {
        $id_halaqah = mysqli_fetch_assoc($cek_lokal)['id'];
    } else {
        mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO halaqah (nama_halaqah) VALUES ('$nama_lokal')");
        $id_halaqah = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
    }

    // Cek duplikasi Username
    $cek_user = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username'");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_user) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Username '$username' sudah digunakan! Silakan gunakan username lain.";
    } else {
        $sql = "INSERT INTO users (username, password, nama_lengkap, jenis_kelamin, role, id_halaqah) 
                VALUES ('$username', '$password', '$nama_lengkap', '$jenis_kelamin', 'ustadz', $id_halaqah)";
        if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql)) {
            $id_baru = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE halaqah SET id_ustadz = $id_baru WHERE id = $id_halaqah");
            $_SESSION['success'] = "$sebutan '$nama_lengkap' berhasil ditambahkan ke $nama_lokal!";
        } else {
            $_SESSION['error'] = "Gagal menyimpan data: " . mysqli_error($conn);
        }
    }
    header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
    exit;
}

// === 2. PROSES EDIT USTADZ / USTADZAH ===
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['edit_ustadz'])) {
    $id_user       = (int) $_POST['id_user'];
    $nama_lengkap  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_lengkap']));
    $jenis_kelamin = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
    $username      = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['username']));
    $nama_lokal    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_lokal']));
    $password_baru = trim($_POST['password_baru'] ?? '');
    $sebutan       = ($jenis_kelamin === 'P') ? 'Ustadzah' : 'Ustadz';

    // Cek username bentrok dengan user lain
    $cek_user = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username' AND id != $id_user");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_user) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Username '$username' sudah dipakai pengguna lain!";
        header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
        exit;
    }

    // Cek / Buat Lokal Mengajar
    $cek_lokal = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM halaqah WHERE nama_halaqah = '$nama_lokal'");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_lokal) > 0) {
        $id_halaqah = mysqli_fetch_assoc($cek_lokal)['id'];
    } else {
        mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO halaqah (nama_halaqah) VALUES ('$nama_lokal')");
        $id_halaqah = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
    }

    $pass_sql = "";
    if (!empty($password_baru)) {
        $hash = password_hash($password_baru, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
        $pass_sql = ", password = '$hash'";
    }

    $sql_update = "UPDATE users SET 
                    nama_lengkap = '$nama_lengkap',
                    jenis_kelamin = '$jenis_kelamin',
                    username = '$username',
                    id_halaqah = $id_halaqah
                    $pass_sql
                   WHERE id = $id_user AND role = 'ustadz'";

    if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql_update)) {
        mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE halaqah SET id_ustadz = $id_user WHERE id = $id_halaqah");
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Data $sebutan '$nama_lengkap' berhasil diperbarui!";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Gagal memperbarui data.";
    }
    header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
    exit;
}

// === 3. PROSES HAPUS USTADZ / USTADZAH ===
if (isset($_GET['hapus'])) {
    $id = (int) $_GET['hapus'];
    mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE halaqah SET id_ustadz = NULL WHERE id_ustadz = $id");
    mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM users WHERE id = $id AND role = 'ustadz'");
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data pengajar (Ustadz/Ustadzah) berhasil dihapus.";
    header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
    exit;
}

// Ambil data seluruh pengajar beserta nama lokalnya
$query_ustadz = "SELECT u.*, h.nama_halaqah 
                 FROM users u 
                 LEFT JOIN halaqah h ON (u.id_halaqah = h.id OR h.id_ustadz = u.id) 
                 WHERE u.role = 'ustadz' 
                 ORDER BY u.nama_lengkap ASC";
$res_ustadz = mysqli_query($conn, $query_ustadz);
$ustadz_list = $res_ustadz ? mysqli_fetch_all($res_ustadz, MYSQLI_ASSOC) : [];

// Hitung Statistik Ustadz & Ustadzah
$total_pengajar = count($ustadz_list);
$total_ustadz   = 0;
$total_ustadzah = 0;
foreach ($ustadz_list as $u) {
    // Deteksi otomatis: jika jenis_kelamin 'P' atau namanya mengandung kata 'ustadzah'
    $is_putri = (($u['jenis_kelamin'] ?? 'L') === 'P' || stripos($u['nama_lengkap'], 'ustadzah') !== false);
    if ($is_putri) {
        $total_ustadzah++;
    } else {
        $total_ustadz++;
    }
}
?>

        }

    <script>
        lucide.createIcons();

        function toggleFormPengajar() {
            const form = document.getElementById('formPengajarContainer');
            form.classList.toggle('hidden');
            if (!form.classList.contains('hidden')) {
                document.getElementById('inputNamaPengajar').focus();
            }
        }

        function updatePrefixPengajar() {
            const jk = document.querySelector('input[name="jenis_kelamin"]:checked').value;
            const passInput = document.getElementById('inputPasswordPengajar');
            if (passInput.value === 'ustadz123' || passInput.value === 'ustadzah123') {
                passInput.value = (jk === 'P') ? 'ustadzah123' : 'ustadz123';
            }
            generateUsernamePengajar();
        }

        function generateUsernamePengajar() {
            const nama = document.getElementById('inputNamaPengajar').value;
            const jk = document.querySelector('input[name="jenis_kelamin"]:checked').value;
            const prefix = (jk === 'P') ? 'ustadzah_' : 'ustadz_';

            // Hilangkan kata sapaan ustadz/ustadzah dari nama agar tidak dobel di username
            const bersih = nama.toLowerCase()
                .replace(/\bustadzah\b/g, '')
                .replace(/\bustadz\b/g, '')
                .replace(/\bust\b/g, '')
                .replace(/[^a-z0-9\s]/g, '')
                .trim();

            const kataPertama = bersih.split(/\s+/)[0] || '';
            document.getElementById('inputUsernamePengajar').value = kataPertama ? prefix + kataPertama : '';
        }

        function filterPengajar() {
            const keyword = document.getElementById('cariPengajar').value.toLowerCase();
            const kat = document.getElementById('filterKategori').value;
            const baris = document.querySelectorAll('.baris-pengajar');
            let tampil = 0;

            baris.forEach(tr => {
                const cocokKata = tr.dataset.search.includes(keyword);
                const cocokKat  = (kat === 'ALL' || tr.dataset.jk === kat);
                if (cocokKata && cocokKat) {
                    tr.style.display = '';
                    tampil++;
                    tr.querySelector('.nomor-urut').innerText = tampil;
                } else {
                    tr.style.display = 'none';
                }
            });
            document.getElementById('jumlahTampil').innerText = tampil;
        }

        function bukaModalEditPengajar(data, jk) {
            document.getElementById('edit_id_user').value = data.id;
            document.getElementById('edit_jk_pengajar').value = jk;
            document.getElementById('edit_nama_pengajar').value = data.nama_lengkap || '';
            document.getElementById('edit_lokal_pengajar').value = data.nama_halaqah || '';
            document.getElementById('edit_username_pengajar').value = data.username || '';
            document.getElementById('modalEditPengajar').classList.remove('hidden');
        }

        function tutupModalEditPengajar() {
            document.getElementById('modalEditPengajar').classList.add('hidden');
        }
    </script>
</body>
</html>

<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || $_SESSION['role'] !== 'ortu') {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$id_ortu = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$anak_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id_user_ortu = $id_ortu ORDER BY nama ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

$id_santri_pilih = isset($_GET['id_santri']) ? (int) $_GET['id_santri'] : ($anak_list[0]['id'] ?? 0);
$santri = null;
foreach ($anak_list as $a) {
    if ($a['id'] == $id_santri_pilih) {
        $santri = $a;
        break;
    }
}

$target_data = null;
$ayat_tercapai_target = 0;
$stat_data = ['total_ziyadah' => 0, 'total_murajaah' => 0, 'total_tasmi' => 0];
$riwayat_list = [];
$chart_labels = [];
$chart_ziyadah = [];

if ($santri) {
    $id_santri = $santri['id'];
    $bulan_ini = date('Y-m');

    // Ambil Target Aktif & Progresnya
    $target_data = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM target_hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"));
    if ($target_data) {
        $tgl_mulai = $target_data['tanggal_mulai'];
        $q_capai = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(jumlah_ayat), 0) as capai FROM hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND jenis = 'ziyadah' AND tanggal >= '$tgl_mulai'");
        $ayat_tercapai_target = (int) mysqli_fetch_assoc($q_capai)['capai'];
    }

    // Statistik Bulan Ini
    $stat_query = "SELECT 
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_ziyadah,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_murajaah,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'tasmi' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_tasmi
                   FROM hafalan 
                   WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan_ini'";
    $stat_data = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $stat_query));

    // Grafik 6 Bulan
    for ($i = 5; $i >= 0; $i--) {
        $bulan = date('Y-m', strtotime("-$i months"));
        $chart_labels[] = date('M Y', strtotime("-$i months"));
        $q = "SELECT COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as z FROM hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan'";
        $chart_ziyadah[] = (int) mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $q))['z'];
    }

    // Riwayat 20 Setoran Terakhir
    $riwayat_query = "SELECT h.*, u.nama_lengkap as nama_ustadz 
                      FROM hafalan h 
                      LEFT JOIN users u ON h.id_ustadz = u.id 
                      WHERE h.id_santri = $id_santri 
                      ORDER BY h.tanggal DESC, h.id DESC LIMIT 20";
    $riwayat_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $riwayat_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
}

        function filterRiwayat() {
            const jenis = document.getElementById('filterJenis').value;

                tr.style.display = (jenis === 'ALL' || tr.dataset.jenis === jenis) ? '' : 'none';

FILE: C:\xampp\htdocs\SIRAH\pr_anak.php
PATH: C:\xampp\htdocs\SIRAH\pr_anak.php

<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

// 🔒 Keamanan: Hanya untuk Role Orang Tua (ortu)
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || $_SESSION['role'] !== 'ortu') { 
    header("Location: login.php"); 
    exit; 
}

$id_ortu = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

// Otomatis tambahkan kolom status_ortu di tabel `pr` jika belum ada
$cek_kolom = mysqli_query($conn, "SHOW COLUMNS FROM pr LIKE 'status_ortu'");
if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_kolom) === 0) {
    mysqli_query($conn, "ALTER TABLE pr ADD COLUMN status_ortu ENUM('Belum','Selesai') DEFAULT 'Belum' AFTER deskripsi");
}

// Ambil daftar anak yang terhubung dengan akun orang tua ini
$anak_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id_user_ortu = $id_ortu ORDER BY nama ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

$id_santri_pilih = isset($_GET['id_santri']) ? (int) $_GET['id_santri'] : ($anak_list[0]['id'] ?? 0);
$santri = null;
foreach ($anak_list as $a) {
    if ($a['id'] == $id_santri_pilih) {
        $santri = $a;
        break;
    }
}

// Proses Toggle Status PR ("Sudah Disimak di Rumah" / "Belum")
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST' && isset($_POST['toggle_status_pr']) && $santri) {
    $id_pr = (int) $_POST['id_pr'];
    $status_baru = ($_POST['status_sekarang'] === 'Selesai') ? 'Belum' : 'Selesai';
    
    mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE pr SET status_ortu = '$status_baru' WHERE id = $id_pr AND id_santri = {$santri['id']}");
    $_SESSION['success'] = ($status_baru === 'Selesai') 
        ? "Alhamdulillah, tugas rumah ditandai sudah disimak di rumah!" 
        : "Status tugas rumah dikembalikan menjadi belum disimak.";
    
    header("Location: pr_anak.php?id_santri=" . $santri['id']);
    exit;
}

$pr_list = [];
$total_pr = 0;
$pr_selesai = 0;
$pr_belum = 0;

if ($santri) {
    $q = "SELECT p.*, u.nama_lengkap as nama_ustadz 
          FROM pr p 
          LEFT JOIN users u ON p.id_ustadz = u.id 
          WHERE p.id_santri = {$santri['id']} 
          ORDER BY FIELD(p.status_ortu, 'Belum', 'Selesai'), p.tanggal DESC, p.id DESC";
    $pr_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $q), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

    $total_pr = count($pr_list);
    foreach ($pr_list as $item) {
        if (($item['status_ortu'] ?? 'Belum') === 'Selesai') {
            $pr_selesai++;
        } else {
            $pr_belum++;
        }
    }
}

$persen_selesai = $total_pr > 0 ? round(($pr_selesai / $total_pr) * 100) : 0;

$Nama_Hari_Indo = [
    'Sunday' => 'Minggu', 'Monday' => 'Senin', 'Tuesday' => 'Selasa',
    'Wednesday' => 'Rabu', 'Thursday' => 'Kamis', 'Friday' => 'Jumat', 'Saturday' => 'Sabtu'
];

    <script>
        lucide.createIcons();

        let statusFilterAktif = 'ALL';

        function setFilterPR(status, btn) {
            statusFilterAktif = status;
            document.querySelectorAll('.tab-filter').forEach(b => {
                b.className = 'tab-filter bg-slate-100 hover:bg-slate-200 text-slate-600 text-xs font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-xl transition';
            });
            btn.className = 'tab-filter bg-slate-900 text-white text-xs font-bold px-4 py-2 rounded-xl transition';
            terapkanFilterPR();
        }

        function terapkanFilterPR() {
            const keyword = document.getElementById('cariPR').value.toLowerCase();
            document.querySelectorAll('.kartu-pr').forEach(card => {
                const cocokStatus = (statusFilterAktif === 'ALL' || card.dataset.status === statusFilterAktif);
                const cocokKata   = card.dataset.search.includes(keyword);
                card.style.display = (cocokStatus && cocokKata) ? '' : 'none';
            });
        }
    </script>
</body>

<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

// 🔒 Keamanan: Hanya Ustadz (dan Pimpinan jika mau)
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$id_user = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];
$role = $_SESSION['role'];

// 1. Ambil daftar santri
// Untuk sementara, tampilkan semua santri di dropdown
// (Nanti bisa difilter berdasarkan halaqah jika tabel halaqah sudah terisi)
$santri_list_query = "SELECT id, nama FROM santri ORDER BY nama ASC";
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $santri_list_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// 2. Tentukan Santri yang Dipilih (Default: Santri pertama di list)
$id_santri_pilih = isset($_GET['id_santri']) ? (int) $_GET['id_santri'] : ($santri_list[0]['id'] ?? 0);
$santri_dipilih = null;
$chart_labels = [];
$chart_ziyadah = [];
$chart_murajaah = [];
$stat = ['total_ayat' => 0, 'total_murajaah' => 0, 'total_tasmi' => 0];
$riwayat_list = [];

if ($id_santri_pilih > 0) {
    // Ambil Detail Santri
    $santri_dipilih = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id = $id_santri_pilih"));
    
    // Ambil Target Terkini
    $target_data = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT target_ayat_per_hari, keterangan FROM target_hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri_pilih ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"));
    $target_ayat = $target_data ? $target_data['target_ayat_per_hari'] : 0;
    $target_ket = $target_data ? $target_data['keterangan'] : 'Belum ada target';

    $bulan_ini = date('Y-m');

    // Statistik Bulan Ini (Khusus Santri Ini)
    $stat_query = "SELECT 
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_ayat,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_murajaah,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'tasmi' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_tasmi
                  FROM hafalan 
                  WHERE id_santri = $id_santri_pilih AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan_ini'";
    $stat = mysqli_fetch_assoc(mysqli_query($conn, $stat_query));

    // Grafik Tren 6 Bulan (Khusus Santri Ini)
    for ($i = 5; $i >= 0; $i--) {
        $bulan = date('Y-m', strtotime("-$i months"));
        $chart_labels[] = date('M Y', strtotime("-$i months"));
        $query = "SELECT 
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as z,
                    COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as m
                  FROM hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri_pilih AND DATE_FORMAT(tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$bulan'";
        $result = mysqli_query($conn, $query);
        $data = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result);
        $chart_ziyadah[] = (int) $data['z'];
        $chart_murajaah[] = (int) $data['m'];
    }

    // Riwayat 10 Terakhir
    $riwayat_query = "SELECT * FROM hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri_pilih ORDER BY tanggal DESC, id DESC LIMIT 10";
    $riwayat_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $riwayat_query), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
}

            </div>

        <!-- STATISTIK BULAN INI -->

<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $id_santri     = (int) $_POST['id_santri'];
    $id_user_ortu  = (int) $_POST['id_user_ortu'];
    $nama_santri   = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_santri']));
    $nis           = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nis']));
    $jk            = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['jenis_kelamin']);
    $tgl_lahir     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['tanggal_lahir']);
    $alamat        = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['alamat']));
    $nama_ortu     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['nama_ortu']));
    $no_hp_ortu    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['no_hp_ortu']));
    $username_ortu = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['username_ortu']));
    $password_baru = trim($_POST['password_baru'] ?? '');

    // 1. Cek duplikasi NIS pada santri lain
    $cek_nis = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM santri WHERE nis = '$nis' AND id != $id_santri");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_nis) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "NIS '$nis' sudah terdaftar pada santri lain!";
        header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
        exit;
    }

    // 2. Update atau buat akun user orang tua
    if ($id_user_ortu > 0) {
        $cek_user = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username_ortu' AND id != $id_user_ortu");
        if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_user) > 0) {
            $_SESSION['error'] = "Username '$username_ortu' sudah dipakai akun lain!";
            header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
            exit;
        }

        if (!empty($password_baru)) {
            $hash = password_hash($password_baru, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE users SET username = '$username_ortu', nama_lengkap = '$nama_ortu', password = '$hash' WHERE id = $id_user_ortu");
        } else {
            mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE users SET username = '$username_ortu', nama_lengkap = '$nama_ortu' WHERE id = $id_user_ortu");
        }
    } elseif (!empty($username_ortu)) {
        // Jika santri lama belum memiliki akun ortu, buatkan otomatis
        $pass_default = !empty($password_baru) ? $password_baru : 'ortu123';
        $hash = password_hash($pass_default, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);
        if (mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO users (username, password, nama_lengkap, role) VALUES ('$username_ortu', '$hash', '$nama_ortu', 'ortu')")) {
            $id_user_ortu = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
        }
    }

    // 3. Update tabel santri
    $id_ortu_sql = $id_user_ortu > 0 ? $id_user_ortu : "NULL";
    $sql_update = "UPDATE santri SET 
                    nama = '$nama_santri',
                    nis = '$nis',
                    jenis_kelamin = '$jk',
                    tanggal_lahir = '$tgl_lahir',
                    alamat = '$alamat',
                    nama_ortu = '$nama_ortu',
                    no_hp_ortu = '$no_hp_ortu',
                    id_user_ortu = $id_ortu_sql
                   WHERE id = $id_santri";

    if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql_update)) {
        $_SESSION['success'] = "Data santri '$nama_santri' berhasil diperbarui!";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "Gagal memperbarui data: " . mysqli_error($conn);
    }

    header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
    exit;
}
?>

<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

if (isset($_GET['id'])) {
    $id_santri = (int) $_GET['id'];

   
    $query_cari = "SELECT id_user_ortu FROM santri WHERE id = $id_santri";
    $result = mysqli_query($conn, $query_cari);
    $data = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result);
    $id_user_ortu = $data['id_user_ortu'];

 
    if (mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM santri WHERE id = $id_santri")) {
        
    
        if ($id_user_ortu) {
            mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM users WHERE id = $id_user_ortu AND role = 'ortu'");
        }
        
        $_SESSION['success'] = "🗑️ Data santri dan akun ortu berhasil dihapus.";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Gagal menghapus data santri.";
    }
}

header("Location: kelola_santri.php");
exit;
?>

            VALUES ($id_santri, '$tanggal', '$jenis', '$surat', $ayat_awal, $ayat_akhir, $jumlah_ayat, '$predikat', '$catatan', $id_ustadz)";

        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Gagal menyimpan data: " . mysqli_error($conn);
