FILE: C:\xampp\htdocs\SIRAH\proses_input_rapor.php
PATH: C:\xampp\htdocs\SIRAH\proses_input_rapor.php
================================================================================
<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $id_santri    = (int) $_POST['id_santri'];
    $kelas        = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['kelas']));
    $judul_materi = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['judul_materi']));
    $semester     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['semester']));
    $tahun_ajaran = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['tahun_ajaran']));
    $juz          = (int) $_POST['juz'];
    $id_ustadz    = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

    // 1. Cek apakah Rapor Header sudah ada
    $cek_query = "SELECT id FROM rapor_tahfidz WHERE id_santri = $id_santri AND juz = $juz AND semester = '$semester' AND tahun_ajaran = '$tahun_ajaran'";
    $cek_result = mysqli_query($conn, $cek_query);

    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_result) > 0) {
        // Jika ada, Update Header & Hapus Detail Lama
        $row = mysqli_fetch_assoc($cek_result);
        $id_rapor = $row['id'];
        mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE rapor_tahfidz SET kelas = '$kelas', judul_materi = '$judul_materi', id_ustadz = $id_ustadz WHERE id = $id_rapor");
        mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM rapor_tahfidz_detail WHERE id_rapor = $id_rapor"); // Reset detail
    } else {
        // Jika belum, Insert Header Baru
        mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO rapor_tahfidz (id_santri, kelas, judul_materi, semester, tahun_ajaran, juz, id_ustadz) 
                             VALUES ($id_santri, '$kelas', '$judul_materi', '$semester', '$tahun_ajaran', $juz, $id_ustadz)");
        $id_rapor = mysqli_insert_id($conn);
    }

    // 2. Insert Detail Materi & Nilai (Dinamis sesuai jumlah kotak)
    if (isset($_POST['materi']) && isset($_POST['nilai'])) {
        $materi_arr = $_POST['materi'];
        $nilai_arr  = $_POST['nilai'];
        $urutan = 1;

        for ($i = 0; $i < count($materi_arr); $i++) {
            $nama_materi = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($materi_arr[$i]));
            $nilai = $nilai_arr[$i] !== '' ? (int) $nilai_arr[$i] : 'NULL';
            
            if (!empty($nama_materi)) {
                mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO rapor_tahfidz_detail (id_rapor, urutan, nama_materi, nilai) 
                                     VALUES ($id_rapor, $urutan, '$nama_materi', $nilai)");
                $urutan++;
            }
        }
    }

    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data nilai rapor berhasil disimpan dengan " . (isset($urutan) ? $urutan-1 : 0) . " materi!";
    header("Location: input_rapor.php?id_santri=$id_santri&juz=$juz&semester=" . urlencode($semester) . "&tahun_ajaran=" . urlencode($tahun_ajaran));
    exit;
}
?>

<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] !== 'POST') {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$username = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['username']);
$password = $_POST['password'];

$query = "SELECT * FROM users WHERE username = '$username'";
$result = mysqli_query($conn, $query);

if (mysqli_num_rows($result) === 1) {
    $user = mysqli_fetch_assoc($result);

    if (password_verify($password, $user['password'])) {
        $_SESSION['user_id']      = $user['id'];
        $_SESSION['username']     = $user['username'];
        $_SESSION['nama_lengkap'] = $user['nama_lengkap'];
        $_SESSION['role']         = $user['role'];

        header("Location: dashboard.php");
        exit;
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Password salah!";
        header("Location: login.php");
        exit;
    }
} else {
    $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Username tidak ditemukan!";
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}
?>

<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || $_SESSION['role'] !== 'pimpinan') {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $nama_lengkap = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['nama_lengkap']);
    $username = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['username']);
    $password = $_POST['password'];
    $role = 'ustadz'; 

    $check = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM users WHERE username = '$username'");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($check) > 0) {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Username '$username' sudah digunakan! Silakan gunakan username lain.";
        header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
        exit;
    }

    $password_hash = password_hash($password, PASSWORD_DEFAULT);

    $query = "INSERT INTO users (username, password, nama_lengkap, role) 
              VALUES ('$username', '$password_hash', '$nama_lengkap', '$role')";
    
    if (mysqli_query($conn, $query)) {
        $_SESSION['success'] = "✅ Ustadz/Ustadzah '$nama_lengkap' berhasil ditambahkan!";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Gagal menambahkan data: " . mysqli_error($conn);
    }
    
    header("Location: kelola-ustadz.php");
    exit;
}

<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

// 🔒 Keamanan: Hanya Ustadz dan Pimpinan
if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    // 1. Ambil dan bersihkan data
    $id_santri      = (int) $_POST['id_santri'];
    $target_ayat    = (int) $_POST['target_ayat'];
    $tanggal_mulai  = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['tanggal_mulai']);
    $keterangan     = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['keterangan']);

    // 2. Cek apakah santri ini sudah punya target sebelumnya
    $cek_query = "SELECT id FROM target_hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1";
    $cek_result = mysqli_query($conn, $cek_query);

    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek_result) > 0) {
        // SUDAH ADA -> Lakukan UPDATE
        $data = mysqli_fetch_assoc($cek_result);
        $id_target = $data['id'];

        $sql = "UPDATE target_hafalan 
                SET target_ayat_per_hari = $target_ayat, 
                    tanggal_mulai = '$tanggal_mulai', 
                    keterangan = '$keterangan' 
                WHERE id = $id_target";
        
        $pesan = "Target santri berhasil diperbarui!";
    } else {
        // BELUM ADA -> Lakukan INSERT
        $sql = "INSERT INTO target_hafalan (id_santri, tanggal_mulai, target_ayat_per_hari, keterangan) 
                VALUES ($id_santri, '$tanggal_mulai', $target_ayat, '$keterangan')";
        
        $pesan = "Target santri baru berhasil ditetapkan!";
    }

    // 3. Eksekusi Query
    if (mysqli_query($conn, $sql)) {
        $_SESSION['success'] = "✅ $pesan";
    } else {
        $_SESSION['error'] = "❌ Gagal menyimpan target: " . mysqli_error($conn);
    }

    // 4. Kembali ke halaman target
    header("Location: target_santri.php");
    exit;
} else {
    header("Location: target_santri.php");
    exit;
}

<?php

    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;

$role    = $_SESSION['role'];
$user_id = (int) $_SESSION['user_id'];

// Fungsi konversi angka ke kata Terbilang (sesuai rumus terbilang di Excel JUZ 1)
function terbilang_indonesia($angka) {
    $angka = abs((int) round($angka));
    $baca  = ["", "Satu", "Dua", "Tiga", "Empat", "Lima", "Enam", "Tujuh", "Delapan", "Sembilan", "Sepuluh", "Sebelas"];
    if ($angka === 0) return "Nol";
    
    $terbilang = "";
    if ($angka < 12) {
        $terbilang = " " . $baca[$angka];
    } elseif ($angka < 20) {
        $terbilang = terbilang_indonesia($angka - 10) . " Belas";
    } elseif ($angka < 100) {
        $terbilang = terbilang_indonesia((int)($angka / 10)) . " Puluh" . ($angka % 10 != 0 ? terbilang_indonesia($angka % 10) : "");
    } elseif ($angka < 200) {
        $terbilang = " Seratus" . ($angka - 100 != 0 ? terbilang_indonesia($angka - 100) : "");
    } elseif ($angka < 1000) {
        $terbilang = terbilang_indonesia((int)($angka / 100)) . " Ratus" . ($angka % 100 != 0 ? terbilang_indonesia($angka % 100) : "");
    } elseif ($angka < 2000) {
        $terbilang = " Seribu" . ($angka - 1000 != 0 ? terbilang_indonesia($angka - 1000) : "");
    } elseif ($angka < 1000000) {
        $terbilang = terbilang_indonesia((int)($angka / 1000)) . " Ribu" . ($angka % 1000 != 0 ? terbilang_indonesia($angka % 1000) : "");
    }
    return trim($terbilang);
}

// Ambil daftar santri (Jika role ortu, hanya tampilkan anaknya)
if ($role === 'ortu') {
    $santri_res = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM santri WHERE id_user_ortu = $user_id ORDER BY nama ASC");
} else {
    $santri_res = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM santri ORDER BY nama ASC");
}
$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all($santri_res, MYSQLI_ASSOC);

$id_santri    = isset($_GET['id_santri']) ? (int) $_GET['id_santri'] : ($santri_list[0]['id'] ?? 0);
$juz          = isset($_GET['juz']) ? (int) $_GET['juz'] : 1;

foreach ($santri_list as $s) {
    if ($s['id'] == $id_santri) {
        $santri_aktif = $s;
        break;

}

$header_data = null;
$detail_data = [];
if ($santri_aktif) {
    $sem_esc = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $semester);
    $thn_esc = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $tahun_ajaran);
    $q_header = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT * FROM rapor_tahfidz WHERE id_santri = {$santri_aktif['id']} AND juz = $juz AND semester = '$sem_esc' AND tahun_ajaran = '$thn_esc' LIMIT 1");

$judul_header = $header_data['judul_materi'] ?? "Materi Tahfidz (Juz $juz)";

PATH: C:\xampp\htdocs\SIRAH\rekap_bulanan.php

<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) {
    header("Location: login.php");
    exit;
}

$bulan_dipilih = isset($_GET['bulan']) ? $_GET['bulan'] : date('m');
$tahun_dipilih = isset($_GET['tahun']) ? $_GET['tahun'] : date('Y');
$periode = $tahun_dipilih . '-' . $bulan_dipilih;

$nama_bulan = ['01' => 'Januari', '02' => 'Februari', '03' => 'Maret', '04' => 'April', '05' => 'Mei', '06' => 'Juni', '07' => 'Juli', '08' => 'Agustus', '09' => 'September', '10' => 'Oktober', '11' => 'November', '12' => 'Desember'];

// Hitung total hari kerja di bulan tersebut (asumsi 26 hari)
$total_hari_kerja = 26;

// Query lengkap: hafalan + kehadiran/absensi
$sql_santri = "SELECT s.id, s.nama, s.nis,
              COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN h.jenis = 'ziyadah' THEN h.jumlah_ayat ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_ayat,
              COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN h.jenis = 'murajaah' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_murajaah,
              COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN h.jenis = 'tasmi' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as total_tasmi,
              COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Hadir' OR k.status = 'H' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as hadir,
              COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Sakit' OR k.status = 'S' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as sakit,
              COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Izin' OR k.status = 'I' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as izin,
              COALESCE(SUM(CASE WHEN a.status = 'Alfa' OR k.status = 'A' THEN 1 ELSE 0 END), 0) as alpa
              FROM santri s 
              LEFT JOIN hafalan h ON s.id = h.id_santri AND DATE_FORMAT(h.tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$periode'
              LEFT JOIN absensi a ON s.id = a.id_santri AND DATE_FORMAT(a.tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$periode'
              LEFT JOIN kehadiran k ON s.id = k.id_santri AND DATE_FORMAT(k.tanggal, '%Y-%m') = '$periode'
              GROUP BY s.id 
              ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$data_santri = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $sql_santri), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
?>

<?php
session_start();
require 'config/database.php';

if (!isset($_SESSION['user_id']) || !in_array($_SESSION['role'], ['ustadz', 'pimpinan'])) { 
    header("Location: login.php"); 
    exit; 
}

// Proses Simpan / Update Target
if ($_SERVER['REQUEST_METHOD'] === 'POST') {
    $id_santri    = (int) $_POST['id_santri'];
    $target_ayat  = max(1, (int) $_POST['target_ayat']);
    $target_surat = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['target_surat']));
    $total_ayat   = max(1, (int) $_POST['total_ayat']);
    $tgl_mulai    = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, $_POST['tanggal_mulai']);
    $ket          = mysqli_real_escape_string($conn, trim($_POST['keterangan']));
    
    // Hitung estimasi selesai
    $hari_dibutuhkan = (int) ceil($total_ayat / $target_ayat);
    $tgl_selesai = date('Y-m-d', strtotime($tgl_mulai . " + $hari_dibutuhkan days"));

    $cek = mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id FROM target_hafalan WHERE id_santri = $id_santri");
    if (mysqli_num_rows($cek) > 0) {
        mysqli_query($conn, "UPDATE target_hafalan SET 
            target_ayat_per_hari = $target_ayat, 
            target_surat = '$target_surat', 
            total_ayat_target = $total_ayat, 
            tanggal_mulai = '$tgl_mulai', 
            tanggal_estimasi_selesai = '$tgl_selesai', 
            keterangan = '$ket' 
            WHERE id_santri = $id_santri");
    } else {
        mysqli_query($conn, "INSERT INTO target_hafalan 
            (id_santri, target_ayat_per_hari, target_surat, total_ayat_target, tanggal_mulai, tanggal_estimasi_selesai, keterangan) 
            VALUES ($id_santri, $target_ayat, '$target_surat', $total_ayat, '$tgl_mulai', '$tgl_selesai', '$ket')");
    }
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Target hafalan santri berhasil disimpan!";
    header("Location: target_santri.php"); 
    exit;
}

// Hapus Target jika diminta
if (isset($_GET['hapus'])) {
    $id_hapus = (int) $_GET['hapus'];
    mysqli_query($conn, "DELETE FROM target_hafalan WHERE id = $id_hapus");
    $_SESSION['success'] = "Data target hafalan berhasil dihapus.";
    header("Location: target_santri.php");
    exit;
}

$santri_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, "SELECT id, nama, nis FROM santri ORDER BY nama ASC"), MYSQLI_ASSOC);

// Ambil daftar target beserta progres ziyadah sejak tanggal_mulai target
$sql_target = "SELECT t.*, s.nama, s.nis, s.jenis_kelamin,
               COALESCE((
                   SELECT SUM(h.jumlah_ayat) 
                   FROM hafalan h 
                   WHERE h.id_santri = t.id_santri 
                     AND h.jenis = 'ziyadah' 
                     AND h.tanggal >= t.tanggal_mulai
               ), 0) AS ayat_tercapai
               FROM target_hafalan t 
               JOIN santri s ON t.id_santri = s.id 
               ORDER BY s.nama ASC";
$target_list = mysqli_fetch_all(mysqli_query($conn, $sql_target), MYSQLI_ASSOC);
?>

                        <?php else: ?>

                                $total_target = max(1, (int) $t['total_ayat_target']);
                                $tercapai     = (int) $t['ayat_tercapai'];
                                $persen       = min(100, round(($tercapai / $total_target) * 100));
                                
                                $bar_color = $persen >= 100 ? 'bg-emerald-500' : ($persen >= 50 ? 'bg-teal-500' : 'bg-amber-500');
                            ?>
                            <tr class="hover:bg-slate-50/80 transition">
                                <td class="py-4 px-6">
                                    <p class="font-bold text-slate-900"><?= htmlspecialchars($t['nama']) ?></p>
                                    <p class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">Mulai: <?= date('d M Y', strtotime($t['tanggal_mulai'])) ?></p>
                                </td>

                                <td class="py-4 px-4">
                                    <p class="font-semibold text-slate-800"><?= htmlspecialchars($t['target_surat'] ?: 'Belum ditentukan') ?></p>
                                    <p class="text-xs text-slate-400"><?= htmlspecialchars($t['keterangan'] ?: 'Target ' . $total_target . ' Ayat') ?></p>
                                </td>

                                <td class="py-4 px-4 text-center">
                                    <span class="inline-flex items-center px-2.5 py-1 rounded-lg text-xs font-bold bg-teal-50 text-teal-700 border border-teal-200">
                                        <?= $t['target_ayat_per_hari'] ?> Ayat/Hari
                                    </span>
                                </td>

                                <td class="py-4 px-4">
                                    <div class="flex justify-between text-xs mb-1">
                                        <span class="font-semibold text-slate-700"><?= $tercapai ?> / <?= $total_target ?> Ayat</span>
                                        <span class="font-bold text-teal-700"><?= $persen ?>%</span>
                                    </div>
                                    <div class="w-full h-2 bg-slate-100 rounded-full overflow-hidden border border-slate-200">
                                        <div class="h-full <?= $bar_color ?> transition-all duration-500" style="width: <?= $persen ?>%"></div>
                                    </div>
                                </td>

                                <td class="py-4 px-4">
                                    <?php if (!empty($t['tanggal_estimasi_selesai'])): ?>
                                        <p class="font-semibold text-slate-700 text-xs"><?= date('d M Y', strtotime($t['tanggal_estimasi_selesai'])) ?></p>
                                        <?php
                                            $sisa_hari = ceil((strtotime($t['tanggal_estimasi_selesai']) - time()) / 86400);
                                            if ($persen >= 100) {
                                                echo '<span class="text-[11px] text-emerald-600 font-bold">🎉 Target Tercapai!</span>';
                                            } elseif ($sisa_hari > 0) {
                                                echo '<span class="text-[11px] text-slate-400">' . $sisa_hari . ' hari lagi</span>';
                                            } else {
                                                echo '<span class="text-[11px] text-rose-500 font-semibold">Lewat batas estimasi</span>';
                                            }
                                        ?>
                                    <?php else: ?>
                                        <span class="text-xs text-slate-400">-</span>
                                    <?php endif; ?>
                                </td>

                                <td class="py-4 px-6 text-center">
                                    <div class="flex items-center justify-center gap-1.5">
                                        <button type="button" onclick='muatKeForm(<?= json_encode($t, JSON_HEX_APOS | JSON_HEX_QUOT) ?>)' 
                                            title="Edit Target Ini"
                                            class="p-2 text-amber-600 hover:bg-amber-50 rounded-lg transition">
                                            <i data-lucide="edit-3" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                                        </button>
                                        <a href="target_santri.php?hapus=<?= $t['id'] ?>" 
                                           onclick="return confirm('Hapus target hafalan untuk <?= htmlspecialchars(addslashes($t['nama'])) ?>?')"
                                           title="Hapus Target"
                                           class="p-2 text-rose-600 hover:bg-rose-50 rounded-lg transition">
                                            <i data-lucide="trash-2" class="w-4 h-4"></i>
                                        </a>
                                    </div>
                                </td>
                            </tr>
                            <?php endforeach; ?>
                        <?php endif; ?>
                    </tbody>
                </table>
            </div>
        </div>

    </div>

    <script>
        lucide.createIcons();

        const mapPresetAyat = {
            "Juz 30 (Juz Amma)": 564,
            "Juz 29 (Tabarak)": 431,
            "Juz 1": 148,
            "QS. Al-Baqarah": 286,
            "QS. Al-Kahf": 110,
            "QS. Yasin": 83,
            "QS. Ar-Rahman": 78,
            "QS. Al-Waqi'ah": 96,
            "QS. Al-Mulk": 30,
            "QS. An-Naba'": 40,
            "QS. An-Nazi'at": 46,
            "QS. 'Abasa": 42
        };

        function cekPresetAyat(val) {
            if (mapPresetAyat[val]) {
                document.getElementById('total_ayat').value = mapPresetAyat[val];
                hitungEstimasi();
            }
        }

        function hitungEstimasi() {
            const total = parseInt(document.getElementById('total_ayat').value) || 0;
            const speed = parseInt(document.getElementById('target_ayat').value) || 0;
            const tglMulai = document.getElementById('tgl_mulai').value;

            if (total > 0 && speed > 0 && tglMulai) {
                const hari = Math.ceil(total / speed);
                const tglSelesai = new Date(tglMulai);
                tglSelesai.setDate(tglSelesai.getDate() + hari);

                const opsiTgl = { day: 'numeric', month: 'long', year: 'numeric' };
                document.getElementById('preview_hari').innerText = hari + " Hari";
                document.getElementById('preview_tanggal').innerText = tglSelesai.toLocaleDateString('id-ID', opsiTgl);
            } else {
                document.getElementById('preview_hari').innerText = "- Hari";
                document.getElementById('preview_tanggal').innerText = "Belum dihitung";
            }
        }

        function muatKeForm(data) {
            document.getElementById('id_santri').value = data.id_santri;
            document.getElementById('target_surat').value = data.target_surat || '';
            document.getElementById('total_ayat').value = data.total_ayat_target || '';
            document.getElementById('target_ayat').value = data.target_ayat_per_hari || '';
            document.getElementById('tgl_mulai').value = data.tanggal_mulai || '';
            document.getElementById('keterangan').value = data.keterangan || '';
            
            hitungEstimasi();
            window.scrollTo({ top: 0, behavior: 'smooth' });
        }

        document.getElementById('total_ayat').addEventListener('input', hitungEstimasi);
        document.getElementById('target_ayat').addEventListener('input', hitungEstimasi);
        document.getElementById('tgl_mulai').addEventListener('change', hitungEstimasi);
    </script>
</body>
</html>
